# Ares tags

Sources: the public [Ares documentation](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/), read from the December 2020 build (the `gh-pages` branch of the documentation repository; its `master` branch holds a 2012 snapshot that lacks most pages). Ares is closed source, so every row rests on that documentation alone. Behaviour changes without a tag are in [`ares-bugfixes.md`](ares-bugfixes.md). A tick (✔) marks a tag this engine already reads. Sizes, file lists and dependencies are estimates from file names; effects are unverified until tested with the real game. Rows are grouped by the system they extend, with the same group names as [`phobos-tags.md`](phobos-tags.md) where one fits; the source link names the Ares page a row came from.

## Weapons

| Tag | Section or type | Effect | Source | Files in `code/` | Size | Depends on |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `Action=SellUnit` | SuperWeaponType (rules) | Super weapon action that sells a docked unit; Ares only fixes an internal error when the target is in a tank bunker. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/restored/actionsellunit.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/unit.cpp` | small | - |
| `SubjectToBuildings` | rules: BulletType | Allows solid buildings to block this projectile (default no). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/buildings/solidbuildings.html) | `code/bullettype.cpp`, `code/bullet.cpp` | medium | `SolidHeight` |
| `SolidLevel` | rules: BulletType | Sets the highest building solidity level the shooter treats as destructible in its way, firing anyway and destroying obstacles first; higher-level buildings force the unit to reposition (default 0). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/buildings/solidbuildings.html) | `code/bullettype.cpp`, `code/bullet.cpp`, `code/foot.cpp` | large | `SubjectToBuildings`, `SolidHeight` |
| `SubjectToTrenches` | rules: BulletType | When no, the projectile treats any building with UC.PassThrough above 0 as having 100% pass-through, and yes keeps the normal chance (default yes). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/buildings/urbancombattrenches.html) | `code/bullettype.cpp`, `code/bullet.cpp`, `code/combat.cpp` | small | `UC.PassThrough` |
| `Ammo` | rules: WeaponType | Sets how many rounds of ammunition one shot uses (default 1); 0 uses none and does not reset the reload timer, and the unit waits for reload when it has fewer rounds than required. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/weapons/ammo.html) | `code/weapon.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | medium | `NoAmmoWeapon` |
| ✔ `Anim` | rules: WeaponType | Accepts 1, 8, 16 or 32 muzzle-flash animations and picks one by firing facing using the smallest power of two that is at least 8; fewer than 8 listed means only the first is used. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/weapons/anim.html) | `code/weapon.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | - |
| `Cursor.Attack` | rules: WeaponType | Names the mouse cursor shown when a single selected unit can fire this weapon at a target in range (default `Attack`). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/weapons/cursors.html) | `code/mouse.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | `[MouseCursors]` |
| `Cursor.AttackOutOfRange` | rules: WeaponType | Names the cursor shown when the unit can attack the target but must move into range first (default `AttackOutOfRange`); not used when two or more units are selected. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/weapons/cursors.html) | `code/mouse.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | `[MouseCursors]` |
| `ApplyDamage` | rules: WeaponType | Sets whether the weapon deals ordinary damage; defaults to no for `IsSonic=yes` or `UseFireParticles=yes` weapons and yes otherwise, and with no the damage is reduced to 0 so warhead animations do not play. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/weapons/damage.html) | `code/weapon.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | - |

| `IvanBomb.CanDetonateDeathBomb` | rules: WeaponType | Lets players manually detonate death bombs planted by this weapon (default `[CombatDamage]CanDetonateDeathBomb`). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/weapons/ivanbombs.html) | `code/techno.cpp`, `code/mouse.cpp` | small | `IvanBomb.DeathBomb` |

| `Wave.IsLaser` | rules: WeaponType | Enables a previously disabled laser-style wave effect for the weapon; the page notes it renders the wider beam of the two laser flags. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/weapons/waves.html) | `code/wave.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | medium | - |
| `Wave.IsBigLaser` | rules: WeaponType | Enables the second laser-style wave effect, which renders narrower than `Wave.IsLaser` despite its name. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/weapons/waves.html) | `code/wave.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | medium | - |
| `Wave.Color` | rules: WeaponType | Sets three integers added to the wave's color components regardless of pixel color, negatives darken it; defaults are Laser 64,0,96, Sonic 0,0,0 and Magnetron 0,0,0, and hex such as 40h is accepted. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/weapons/waves.html) | `code/wave.cpp` | small | `Wave.Intensity` |
| `Wave.Intensity` | rules: WeaponType | Sets three integers (1/256 units) that scale the original pixel color before it is added, using c + color * x + c * intensity * x; defaults are 0,0,0 for Laser, 0,256,256 for Sonic and 128,0,1024 for Magnetron, but 0,0,0 whenever `Wave.Color` is set. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/weapons/waves.html) | `code/wave.cpp` | small | `Wave.Color` |
| `Wave.IsHouseColor` | rules: WeaponType | Draws the wave in the firer's house color instead of `Wave.Color`. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/weapons/waves.html) | `code/wave.cpp` | small | `Wave.Color` |
| `Wave.ReverseAgainstVehicles` | rules: WeaponType | Draws the wave from target to firer when the target is a vehicle (default yes with `IsMagBeam=yes`, else no). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/weapons/waves.html) | `code/wave.cpp` | small | `IsMagBeam` |
| `Wave.ReverseAgainstBuildings` | rules: WeaponType | Draws the wave from target to firer when the target is a building (default no). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/weapons/waves.html) | `code/wave.cpp` | small | - |
| `Wave.ReverseAgainstInfantry` | rules: WeaponType | Draws the wave from target to firer when the target is infantry (default no). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/weapons/waves.html) | `code/wave.cpp` | small | - |
| `Wave.ReverseAgainstAircraft` | rules: WeaponType | Draws the wave from target to firer when the target is an aircraft (default no). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/weapons/waves.html) | `code/wave.cpp` | small | - |
| `Wave.ReverseAgainstOthers` | rules: WeaponType | Draws the wave from target to firer for any target not covered by the other flags, such as trees, overlays or empty cells (default no). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/weapons/waves.html) | `code/wave.cpp` | small | - |
| ✔ `AmbientDamage` | rules: WeaponType | Sets the damage a wave deals to objects it passes through (default 0); with Ares this works for every wave type, not only sonic waves. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/weapons/waves.html) | `code/wave.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | medium | `Warhead` |
| `IsDetachedRailgun` | rules: WeaponType | Makes the weapon a railgun with the same particles as `IsRailgun` that can fire again before its earlier beam has dissolved (default no); combining it with `IsRailgun=yes` is unsupported. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/weapons/railgun.html) | `code/weapon.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp`, `code/partsys.cpp` | medium | - |
| ✔ `ProjectileRange` | rules: WeaponType | Sets how far a `Ranged=yes` projectile flies before it runs out of fuel and detonates; the page gives the default as 390 and calls it a cell range, but elsewhere says about 390 cells, so the unit is unclear. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/weapons/projectilerange.html) | `code/bullet.cpp`, `code/weapon.cpp` | small | `Ranged` |
| `AnimLength` | rules: BulletType | Sets the frames in each facing's animation for a `Rotates=yes` projectile (default 1), played at one frame per `AnimRate`; `AnimRate=0` crashes the game. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/projectiles/animations.html) | `code/bullet.cpp`, `code/bullettype.cpp` | medium | `Rotates`, `AnimRate` |
| `AttachedSystem` | rules: BulletType | Attaches a particle system to the projectile as a smoke trail; only systems with `BehavesLike=smoke` are supported. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/projectiles/attachedsystem.html) | `code/bullet.cpp`, `code/bullettype.cpp`, `code/partsys.cpp` | medium | - |
| `BallisticScatter.Min` | rules: BulletType | Sets the minimum scatter in cells for the projectile; defaults to `[CombatDamage]BallisticScatter` / 2 when `FlakScatter=no` or `Inviso=yes`, otherwise 0. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/projectiles/ballisticscatter.html) | `code/bullet.cpp`, `code/bullettype.cpp`, `code/rules.cpp` | small | `Inaccurate`, `Arcing` |
| `BallisticScatter.Max` | rules: BulletType | Sets the maximum scatter in cells for the projectile (default `[CombatDamage]BallisticScatter`). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/projectiles/ballisticscatter.html) | `code/bullet.cpp`, `code/bullettype.cpp`, `code/rules.cpp` | small | `Inaccurate`, `Arcing` |
| ✔ `Ranged` | rules: BulletType | Lets the projectile run out of fuel and detonate in mid-air at its weapon's `ProjectileRange` (default no). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/projectiles/ranged.html) | `code/bullet.cpp`, `code/bullettype.cpp` | small | `ProjectileRange` |
| ✔ `Splits` | rules: BulletType | Makes the projectile split into the number of clusters set by `Cluster`, each carrying the `AirburstWeapon`, when it airbursts, exceeds its range or otherwise detonates (default no); retargets only air units if `AA=yes` and only objects the warhead affects. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/projectiles/splits.html) | `code/bullet.cpp`, `code/bullettype.cpp`, `code/missile.cpp` | large | `Cluster`, `AirburstWeapon`, `Airburst`, `Ranged` |
| ✔ `RetargetAccuracy` | rules: BulletType | Sets the chance, 0.0 to 1.0 (default 0.0), that a split cluster aims at the original target instead of picking another. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/projectiles/splits.html) | `code/bullet.cpp`, `code/bullettype.cpp` | small | `Splits` |
| `RetargetSelf` | rules: BulletType | Allows the firer itself to be chosen as a retarget (default yes); with no the firer is skipped but can still be hit by blast on its cell. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/projectiles/splits.html) | `code/bullet.cpp`, `code/bullettype.cpp` | small | `Splits` |
| `AirburstSpread` | rules: BulletType | Sets the radius in cells of the airburst area, each cell in range being hit by the `AirburstWeapon` (default 1.5); needs `Airburst=yes` and cannot be combined with `Splits=yes`. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/projectiles/splits.html) | `code/bullet.cpp`, `code/bullettype.cpp` | small | `Airburst`, `AirburstWeapon` |
| `AroundTarget` | rules: BulletType | Makes clusters of a `Splits=yes` or `Airburst=yes` projectile search for new targets around the original target and keep flying to it, rather than around the split point (default same as `Splits`). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/projectiles/splits.html) | `code/bullet.cpp`, `code/bullettype.cpp` | small | `Splits`, `Airburst` |
| `Abductor` | rules: WeaponType | Hits absorb the target into the attacker's passenger hold, releasing them when the attacker dies, and fall back to normal damage when the hold is full or the target cannot be taken; default no. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/chronoprisons.html) | `code/weapon.cpp`, `code/bullet.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | large | `Passengers`, `SizeLimit`, `PipScale` |
| `Abductor.Temporal` | rules: WeaponType | Abducts the target only after it has been temporally erased, placing it in the attacker; if that fails the target is erased as usual, and the warhead needs `Temporal=yes`. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/chronoprisons.html) | `code/weapon.cpp`, `code/temporal.cpp` | medium | `Abductor` |
| `Abductor.Anim` | rules: WeaponType | Animation spawned where a unit is abducted; defaults to none. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/chronoprisons.html) | `code/weapon.cpp`, `code/anim.cpp` | small | `Abductor` |
| `Abductor.ChangeOwner` | rules: WeaponType | Boolean that gives the abducted unit to the abductor's house, except units that are `ImmuneToPsionics=yes` or have the PSIONICSIMMUNE ability; defaults to no. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/chronoprisons.html) | `code/weapon.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | `Abductor` |
| `Abductor.AbductBelowPercent` | rules: WeaponType | Health percentage a target must be below to be abducted; defaults to 100%. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/chronoprisons.html) | `code/weapon.cpp` | small | `Abductor` |
| `Abductor.MaxHealth` | rules: WeaponType | Hitpoints above which a unit cannot be abducted; 0 (default) skips the check. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/chronoprisons.html) | `code/weapon.cpp` | small | `Abductor` |

## Warheads

| Tag | Section or type | Effect | Source | Files in `code/` | Size | Depends on |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `[ArmorTypes]` entries (`name=<existing armor or N%>`) | rules: `[ArmorTypes]` section | Declares new armor names whose per-warhead damage multiplier starts out equal to an existing armor or to a flat percentage. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/additionalarmortypesandverses.html) | `code/warhead.cpp`, `code/objtype.cpp`, `code/rules.cpp` | large | - |
| `IsFlammable` (forest fires) | rules: TerrainType | Ares requires this on each tree that Sparky warheads may ignite, so fire spreads only to flagged trees. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/forestfires.html) | `code/terrain.cpp`, `code/terrtype.cpp` | small | `Sparky`, `OnFire`, `TreeFire` |
| `EMP.Duration` | WarheadType (rules) | Number of frames of EMP applied to a target hit by the warhead; negative values shorten an existing EMP. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/restored/emp.html) | `code/warhead.cpp`, `code/empulse.cpp` | medium | `EMP.Cap` |
| `EMP.Cap` | WarheadType (rules) | Selects how EMP.Duration combines with the target's remaining EMP time: set-to, stack with ceiling, or stack without limit. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/restored/emp.html) | `code/warhead.cpp`, `code/empulse.cpp` | medium | `EMP.Duration` |
| `EMP.Sparkles` | WarheadType (rules) | Animation used on targets without their own EMP animation, overriding the default. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/restored/emp.html) | `code/warhead.cpp`, `code/empulse.cpp` | small | `EMP.Duration` |
| `AffectsEnemies` | rules: WarheadType | Sets whether the warhead can damage enemy units, the counterpart of `AffectsAllies`; it does not change whether the weapon may target enemies. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/general.html) | `code/warhead.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | `AffectsAllies` |
| `AffectsOwner` | rules: WarheadType | Sets whether the warhead can affect the player who fired it (default same as `AffectsAllies`). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/general.html) | `code/warhead.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | `AffectsAllies` |
| `EffectsRequireDamage` | rules: WarheadType | Applies KillDriver, Sonar, DisableWeapons and Flash only if at least 1 hitpoint of damage was dealt (default no); healing does not use this setting. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/general.html) | `code/warhead.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | `Sonar.Duration`, `DisableWeapons.Duration`, `Flash.Duration` |
| `EffectsRequireVerses` | rules: WarheadType | Applies those effects only where the Verses value is above 0% (default yes); with no they apply regardless of Verses. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/general.html) | `code/warhead.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | `EffectsRequireDamage` |
| `AllowZeroDamage` | rules: WarheadType | Passes 0 damage on to all affected objects so effects still apply, instead of skipping the area search (default no). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/general.html) | `code/combat.cpp`, `code/warhead.cpp` | small | `EffectsRequireDamage` |
| `Malicious` | rules: WarheadType | Controls whether a hit triggers the `EVA_OreMinerUnderAttack` notice for ore miners (default yes); other EVA messages are not suppressed. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/general.html) | `code/techno.cpp`, `code/warhead.cpp`, `code/vox.cpp` | small | - |
| `PreventScatter` | rules: WarheadType | Stops units from scattering when hit by this warhead even if they normally would (default no). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/general.html) | `code/techno.cpp`, `code/warhead.cpp` | small | - |
| ✔ `CellSpread` | rules: WarheadType | Accepts values larger than the stock limit of 11 because Ares replaces the cell lookup table; area-of-effect Ares features follow it, and the affected cells grow with the square of the range. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/cellspread.html) | `code/combat.cpp`, `code/warhead.cpp` | medium | - |
| `CellSpread.MaxAffect` | rules: WarheadType | Sets how many times one object, such as a multi-cell building, can be hit by one blast, keeping the hits closest to the impact (default -1 for unlimited; 1 hits it once). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/cellspread.html) | `code/combat.cpp`, `code/warhead.cpp` | small | `CellSpread` |
| `Culling.RookieBelowHealth` | rules: WarheadType | Sets the health percentage (1 to 100, or 0 critical, -1 damaged, -2 healthy) below which a rookie firer's hit culls the victim (default 0). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/culling.html) | `code/parasite.cpp`, `code/warhead.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | medium | `Culling` |
| `Culling.VeteranBelowHealth` | rules: WarheadType | Sets the same health threshold for a veteran firer (default 0, critical). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/culling.html) | `code/parasite.cpp`, `code/warhead.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | medium | `Culling` |
| `Culling.EliteBelowHealth` | rules: WarheadType | Sets the same health threshold for an elite firer (default -1, damaged). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/culling.html) | `code/parasite.cpp`, `code/warhead.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | medium | `Culling` |
| `Culling.Chance` | rules: WarheadType | Sets the percent chance that a cullable victim is culled for every veterancy level (default 100); the roll repeats on each hit, not once per victim. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/culling.html) | `code/parasite.cpp`, `code/warhead.cpp` | small | `Culling` |
| `Culling.RookieChance` | rules: WarheadType | Sets the cull chance in percent for a rookie firer (default 100). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/culling.html) | `code/parasite.cpp`, `code/warhead.cpp` | small | `Culling` |
| `Culling.VeteranChance` | rules: WarheadType | Sets the cull chance in percent for a veteran firer (default 100). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/culling.html) | `code/parasite.cpp`, `code/warhead.cpp` | small | `Culling` |
| `Culling.EliteChance` | rules: WarheadType | Sets the cull chance in percent for an elite firer (default 100). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/culling.html) | `code/parasite.cpp`, `code/warhead.cpp` | small | `Culling` |
| `DamageAirThreshold` | rules: WarheadType | Damages airborne units only if the warhead detonates more than this many leptons above the ground (default 0); -1 also hits air units on a ground detonation and 9999 effectively disables air damage. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/damageair.html) | `code/combat.cpp`, `code/warhead.cpp` | small | - |
| `DamageAirConsiderBridges` | rules: [General] | Treats a detonation on a bridge as at the bridge's height for `DamageAirThreshold` (default no, where it counts as 416 leptons, four levels, above ground). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/damageair.html) | `code/combat.cpp`, `code/rules.cpp` | small | `DamageAirThreshold` |
| `DeathWeapon.Suppress` | rules: WarheadType | Lists TechnoTypes whose death weapon does not fire when this warhead kills them; damage that merely weakens them has no effect. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/deathweapon.html) | `code/warhead.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | - |
| `DeathWeapon.SuppressVehicles` | rules: WarheadType | Suppresses the death weapon of every vehicle this warhead kills (default no). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/deathweapon.html) | `code/warhead.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | `DeathWeapon.Suppress` |
| `DeathWeapon.SuppressInfantry` | rules: WarheadType | Suppresses the death weapon of every infantry unit this warhead kills (default no). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/deathweapon.html) | `code/warhead.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | `DeathWeapon.Suppress` |
| `DieSound.Override` | rules: WarheadType | Replaces the die sound of objects this warhead destroys; `<none>` silences it, and objects without a DieSound play nothing. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/diesound.html) | `code/warhead.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | - |
| `VoiceDie.Override` | rules: WarheadType | Replaces the death voice of objects this warhead destroys; `<none>` silences it, and objects without a VoiceDie play nothing. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/diesound.html) | `code/warhead.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | - |
| `DisableWeapons.Duration` | rules: WarheadType | Stops the target firing its weapons for this many frames if positive (default 0); it can still reload and target, and death weapons and C4 still work. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/disableweapons.html) | `code/warhead.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | medium | `EffectsRequireDamage` |
| `Flash.Duration` | rules: WarheadType | Makes the victim flash for this many frames if positive (default 0); stock repair and heal use 7 frames. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/flash.html) | `code/warhead.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | `EffectsRequireDamage` |
| `Damage.Deployed` | rules: WarheadType | Multiplies damage to deployed infantry, separate from `ProneDamage` (default 100%). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/infantry.html) | `code/warhead.cpp`, `code/infantry.cpp` | small | - |
| `InfDeathAnim` | rules: WarheadType | Names the animation shown when this warhead kills an infantry unit with `NotHuman=no`, and detects mutation animations automatically, allowing any number of mutations. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/infantry.html) | `code/warhead.cpp`, `code/infantry.cpp` | medium | `MakeInfantryOwner` |

| `IonCannon.Blast` | rules: WarheadType | Names the first impact animation, used only on land (on water the last `[CombatDamage]SplashList` animation plays); `none` disables it (default `[General]IonBlast`). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/ioncannon.html) | `code/ionblast.cpp`, `code/warhead.cpp` | small | `IonCannon` |
| `IonCannon.Beam` | rules: WarheadType | Names the second impact animation; `none` disables it (default `[General]IonBeam`). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/ioncannon.html) | `code/ionblast.cpp`, `code/warhead.cpp` | small | `IonCannon` |
| `IonCannon.Warhead` | rules: WarheadType | Names the warhead that delivers the Ion Cannon damage; `none` deals no damage (default `[CombatDamage]IonCannonWarhead`). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/ioncannon.html) | `code/ionblast.cpp`, `code/warhead.cpp`, `code/rules.cpp` | small | `IonCannon` |

| `IonCannon.Rock` | rules: WarheadType | Sets whether voxel units are rocked by the impact (default yes). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/ioncannon.html) | `code/ionblast.cpp`, `code/warhead.cpp` | small | `IonCannon` |
| `IonCannon.Ripple` | rules: WarheadType | Draws the Tiberian Sun Ion Cannon shockwave visual for 0 (off) to 79 frames of effect strength (default 79 with `IonCannon=yes`, else 0); the effect is visual only. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/ioncannon.html) | `code/ionblast.cpp`, `code/tactical.cpp` | medium | - |
| `BridgeAbsoluteDestroyer` | rules: WarheadType | Makes the warhead destroy a bridge it hits outright (default yes only for `[CombatDamage]IonCannonWarhead`); needs `Wall=yes`. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/ioncannon.html) | `code/combat.cpp`, `code/warhead.cpp` | small | `Wall` |
| `IronCurtain.Duration` | rules: WarheadType | Gives the target the Iron Curtain effect for this many frames if positive, or shortens its remaining time if negative (default 0); damage is dealt first, and infantry and `Organic=yes` units die instantly. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/ironcurtain.html) | `code/warhead.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp`, `code/combat.cpp` | medium | `IronCurtain.Cap`, `IronCurtain.Modifier` |
| `IronCurtain.Cap` | rules: WarheadType | Controls stacking of `IronCurtain.Duration`: negative (default -1) makes it absolute, 0 lets it stack without limit, and a positive value caps the total, without cutting a longer existing duration. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/ironcurtain.html) | `code/warhead.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | `IronCurtain.Duration` |
| `IronCurtain.Modifier` | rules: TechnoType | Multiplies a positive Iron Curtain duration applied to this object (default 100%); 0% makes it immune. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/ironcurtain.html) | `code/techtype.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | `IronCurtain.Duration` |

| `IronCurtainFlash` | rules: [AudioVisual] | Sets the default for whether Iron Curtained or force-shielded objects flash when hit (default yes). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/ironcurtain.html) | `code/rules.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | `IronCurtain.Flash` |
| `MindControl.Permanent` | rules: WarheadType | Makes the mind control of a `MindControl=yes` warhead permanent, behaving like the Psychic Dominator: it re-controls already controlled units, has no unit limit, and affects buildings the warhead can target. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/mindcontrol.html) | `code/warhead.cpp`, `code/capture.cpp`, `code/psydom.cpp` | medium | `MindControl` |
| `NukeFlash.Duration` | rules: WarheadType | Sets the frames the map-wide flash fades in and holds before fading out; 0 disables it (default 30 for the warhead named NUKE, else 0). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/nukeflash.html) | `code/combat.cpp`, `code/tactical.cpp` | medium | - |
| `PreImpactAnim` | rules: WarheadType | Names a non-looping animation shown at impact before the explosion and damage happen (default NUKEBALL for NUKE, else none). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/preimpactanim.html) | `code/bullet.cpp`, `code/warhead.cpp`, `code/anim.cpp` | medium | - |

| `[ArmorTypes]` | rules: new section | Declares extra armor types; `name=armor` copies that armor's Verses on every warhead and `name=NN%` gives every warhead that flat Verses by default, and objects then use the name in `Armor=` case-insensitively. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/additionalarmortypesandverses.html) | `code/armortypes.cpp`, `code/rules.cpp`, `code/warhead.cpp` | medium | - |
| `Versus.<armor>` | rules: WarheadType | Damage modifier (float) of this warhead against the custom armor type named in the suffix; the original 11 armors keep using `Verses`. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/additionalarmortypesandverses.html) | `code/warhead.cpp`, `code/armortypes.cpp` | medium | `[ArmorTypes]` |
| `Versus.<armor>.ForceFire` | rules: WarheadType | Boolean that allows or forbids force-firing this warhead at objects of that armor type, independent of the damage modifier. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/additionalarmortypesandverses.html) | `code/warhead.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | `Versus.<armor>` |

| ✔ `Verses` | rules: WarheadType | The special values 0%, 1% and 2% in the original 11-value list switch off force fire plus retaliation plus passive acquisition, retaliation plus passive acquisition, and passive acquisition only; a list shorter than 11 values no longer crashes the parser. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/additionalarmortypesandverses.html) | `code/warhead.cpp` | small | - |
| ✔ `AttachEffect.Animation` | rules: TechnoType, WarheadType | Optional animation attached to the affected unit and looped until the effect ends (`LoopCount` ignored); it is removed while the unit is cloaked, and the effect itself stays applied. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/attacheffect.html) | `code/attacheffect.cpp`, `code/anim.cpp`, `code/warhead.cpp` | large | `AttachEffect.Duration` |

| ✔ `AttachEffect.ROFMultiplier` | rules: TechnoType, WarheadType | Rate-of-fire multiplier while the effect lasts, applied once when a reload starts so a long reload can outlast the effect; defaults to 1.0. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/attacheffect.html) | `code/attacheffect.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | medium | - |
| ✔ `AttachEffect.Cloakable` | rules: TechnoType, WarheadType | Boolean that gives the unit cloaking ability while the effect lasts; defaults to no. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/attacheffect.html) | `code/attacheffect.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | medium | - |

| ✔ `AttachEffect.PenetratesIronCurtain` | rules: TechnoType, WarheadType | Boolean that lets the effect attach to a unit or structure under an Iron Curtain or Force Shield; defaults to no. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/attacheffect.html) | `code/attacheffect.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | - |

| `KillDriver` | rules: WarheadType | Makes the warhead kill the driver of a vehicle instead of damaging it; the driver is the first passenger matching the vehicle's Operator and all other passengers are ejected (default no). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/killingdrivers.html) | `code/warhead.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp`, `code/unit.cpp` | large | Operator, ProtectedDriver, KillDriver.Owner |

## Superweapons

| Tag | Section or type | Effect | Source | Files in `code/` | Size | Depends on |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `SW.Range` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Sets the affected area as one float (circle radius in cells, no longer limited to cell spread's maximum of 10) or two integers (rectangle width,height); the default depends on Type (ChronoSphere 3,3; LightningStorm [General]LightningCellSpread; GeneticConverter 3,3; IronCurtain 3,3; ForceShield [General]ForceShieldRadius; PsychicDominator [General]DominatorCaptureRange; PsychicReveal [CombatDamage]PsychicRevealRadius capped at 10; SonarPulse 10) and -1 means the whole map for PsychicReveal and SonarPulse. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/general.html) | `code/suprtype.cpp`, `code/super.cpp` | medium | - |
| `SW.Deferment` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Sets how many frames pass between firing and the effect; only some types support it (LightningStorm defaults to [General]LightningDeferment, PsychicDominator to 0, UnitDelivery to 20). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/general.html) | `code/suprtype.cpp`, `code/super.cpp` | small | - |
| `SW.CreateRadarEvent` | rules: SuperWeaponType | If yes, every player gets a radar event rectangle centred on the target cell when the super weapon fires. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/general.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/radar.cpp` | small | - |
| `SW.ShowCameo` | rules: SuperWeaponType | If no, the super weapon has no sidebar cameo (default yes); the page says the setting is ignored when SW.AutoFire=no. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/general.html) | `code/sidebar.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | small | `SW.AutoFire` |
| `SW.TimerVisibility` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Chooses who sees the super weapon timer when ShowTimer=yes (none, owner, allies, team, enemies or all; observers count as allied to everyone); default all. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/general.html) | `code/tactical.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | small | `ShowTimer` |
| `SW.Group` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Integer (default 0) that tells apart several super weapons of the same Type, for example to match an Iron Curtain team script action to a specific Iron Curtain. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/general.html) | `code/suprtype.cpp`, `code/team.cpp` | small | - |
| `SW.AffectsHouse` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Chooses whose objects the effect hits (none, owner, allies, team, enemies, all, or a comma list; team means owner plus allies); default team for ForceShield and all otherwise, with enemies for HunterSeeker and SonarPulse. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/general.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | medium | - |
| `SW.AffectsTarget` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Limits the effect to listed target kinds (land, water, empty, infantry, units, buildings, or a comma list); with no land or water entry both are allowed, with no other entry everything is allowed, and the default depends on Type (infantry,units for ChronoSphere and PsychicDominator; buildings for ForceShield; water for SonarPulse). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/general.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | medium | - |
| `SW.InitialReady` | rules: SuperWeaponType | If yes, the first super weapon of this Type is ready to fire as soon as it becomes available, then recharges normally; default no. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/general.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/house.cpp` | small | - |
| `SW.VirtualCharge` | rules: SuperWeaponType | If yes, the super weapon keeps charging after its last providing building is lost and returns with the charge progress kept; default no and not supported with UseChargeDrain=yes. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/general.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/house.cpp`, `code/saveload.cpp` | medium | `UseChargeDrain` |
| `SW.RequiredHouses` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Lists the houses allowed to get this super weapon; all other houses never see it (default all houses). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/availability.html) | `code/house.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | small | - |
| `SW.ForbiddenHouses` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Lists houses that can never get this super weapon; other houses must still satisfy SW.RequiredHouses (default none). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/availability.html) | `code/house.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | small | `SW.RequiredHouses` |
| `SW.AuxBuildings` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Lists building types of which the player must own at least one for the super weapon to become available; replaces the single AuxBuilding and defaults to none. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/availability.html) | `code/house.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | small | - |
| `SW.NegBuildings` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Lists building types whose presence makes the super weapon unavailable; it is available only if the player owns none of them (default none). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/availability.html) | `code/house.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | small | - |
| `AuxBuilding` | rules: SuperWeaponType | The original single auxiliary building now also gates super weapons provided by building upgrades, but an upgrade never counts as a prerequisite for it or for SW.AuxBuildings and SW.NegBuildings. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/availability.html) | `code/suprtype.cpp`, `code/building.cpp` | small | `SW.AuxBuildings` |
| `SW.AllowPlayer` | rules: SuperWeaponType | If no, human players cannot get this super weapon (default yes). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/availability.html) | `code/house.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | small | - |
| `SW.AllowAI` | rules: SuperWeaponType | If no, AI players cannot get this super weapon (default yes). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/availability.html) | `code/house.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | small | - |
| `SW.Shots` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Number of times the super weapon may fire before it becomes unavailable and its cameo disappears; -1 means unlimited (default) and Charge Drain super weapons are not supported; super weapons granted by crates or map actions also count. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/availability.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/saveload.cpp` | medium | - |
| `SW.AlwaysGranted` | rules: SuperWeaponType | If yes, the super weapon needs no providing building and is available to every undefeated player, still subject to the aux building, house and player/AI restrictions (default no). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/availability.html) | `code/house.cpp`, `code/super.cpp` | medium | `SW.AuxBuildings`, `SW.NegBuildings`, `SW.RequiredHouses`, `SW.ForbiddenHouses`, `SW.AllowPlayer`, `SW.AllowAI` |
| `Text.Hold` | rules: SuperWeaponType | CSF label overlaid on the sidebar cameo when the super weapon is powered but cannot charge because the building is shut down. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/cameooverlay.html) | `code/sidebar.cpp`, `code/csf.cpp` | small | - |
| `Text.Ready` | rules: SuperWeaponType | CSF label overlaid on the cameo when the super weapon is fully charged (HunterSeeker defaults to TXT_RELEASE). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/cameooverlay.html) | `code/sidebar.cpp`, `code/csf.cpp` | small | - |
| `Text.Charging` | rules: SuperWeaponType | CSF label overlaid on the cameo for a UseChargeDrain=yes super weapon that can be fired but is not fully charged. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/cameooverlay.html) | `code/sidebar.cpp`, `code/csf.cpp` | small | `UseChargeDrain` |
| `Text.Active` | rules: SuperWeaponType | CSF label overlaid on the cameo for a UseChargeDrain=yes super weapon that is enabled and draining. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/cameooverlay.html) | `code/sidebar.cpp`, `code/csf.cpp` | small | `UseChargeDrain` |
| `Text.Preparing` | rules: SuperWeaponType | CSF label overlaid on the cameo whenever none of the other Text.* labels applies, such as charging on a super weapon without charge drain. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/cameooverlay.html) | `code/sidebar.cpp`, `code/csf.cpp` | small | - |
| `SW.ChargeToDrainRatio` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Multiplier on the recharge time that sets how long a charge-drain super weapon stays active; must not be 0 and defaults to [General]ChargeToDrainRatio. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/chargedrain.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | small | `UseChargeDrain` |
| `SW.Unstoppable` | rules: SuperWeaponType | If no (default), clicking the cameo of an active charge-drain super weapon stops it; if yes the click is ignored. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/chargedrain.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/sidebar.cpp` | small | `UseChargeDrain` |
| `Money.Amount` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Credits added to the firing player when the super weapon fires (negative subtracts, default 0); the launch aborts with an EVA event when funds are short, the cost shows in the cameo tooltip and the cameo is grayed out when unaffordable. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/cost.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/house.cpp`, `code/sidebar.cpp` | medium | `EVA.InsufficientFunds` |
| `Money.DrainAmount` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Credits added to the owner each time the drain delay elapses while a UseChargeDrain=yes super weapon is active (negative subtracts, default 0). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/cost.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/house.cpp` | small | `Money.DrainDelay`, `UseChargeDrain` |
| `Money.DrainDelay` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Frames between each Money.DrainAmount payment while a UseChargeDrain=yes super weapon is active (default 0). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/cost.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/house.cpp` | small | `Money.DrainAmount`, `UseChargeDrain` |
| `Cursor` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Mouse cursor shown when the super weapon may fire at the hovered location (default Attack; per-Type defaults include Chronosphere, LightningStorm, Nuke, ParaDrop, SpyPlane, PsychicDominator, PsychicReveal, GeneticMutator, ForceShield and IronCurtain). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/cursors.html) | `code/mouse.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | small | - |
| `NoCursor` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Mouse cursor shown when the super weapon cannot fire there because of target type, owner, range or a missing designator (default Disallowed; AttackOutOfRange for EMPulse, NoForceShield for ForceShield). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/cursors.html) | `code/mouse.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | small | - |
| `EVA.Detected` | rules: SuperWeaponType | EVA event played to other players when the super weapon building is built (DropPod defaults to EVA_DropPodDetected, HunterSeeker to EVA_HunterSeekerDetected); none disables it. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/evaevents.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/vox.cpp` | small | - |
| `EVA.Ready` | rules: SuperWeaponType | EVA event played to the owner when the super weapon is ready (DropPod defaults to EVA_DropPodReady, HunterSeeker to EVA_HunterSeekerReady); none disables it. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/evaevents.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/vox.cpp` | small | - |
| `EVA.Activated` | rules: SuperWeaponType | EVA event played when the super weapon fires (DropPod defaults to EVA_DropPodActivated, HunterSeeker to EVA_HunterSeekerLaunched); none disables it. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/evaevents.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/vox.cpp` | small | - |
| `EVA.Impatient` | rules: SuperWeaponType | EVA event played when the cameo is clicked before the super weapon is ready. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/evaevents.html) | `code/sidebar.cpp`, `code/vox.cpp` | small | - |
| `EVA.InsufficientFunds` | rules: SuperWeaponType | EVA event played when the super weapon cannot fire for lack of money; defaults to EVA_InsufficientFunds. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/evaevents.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/vox.cpp` | small | `Money.Amount` |
| `EVA.SelectTarget` | rules: SuperWeaponType | EVA event played when the cameo is clicked and the player must choose a target. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/evaevents.html) | `code/sidebar.cpp`, `code/vox.cpp` | small | - |
| `SW.PostDependent` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Names the super weapon invoked right after this one fires; in Red Alert 2 only the ChronoSphere uses it to hand over to a ChronoWarp, and it defaults to the first ChronoWarp in SuperWeaponTypes. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/hardcoded.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | small | - |
| `PreClick` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Ares hardcodes this value and ignores whatever the rules file sets. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/hardcoded.html) | `code/suprtype.cpp` | small | `SW.PostDependent` |
| `PostClick` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Ares hardcodes this value and ignores whatever the rules file sets. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/hardcoded.html) | `code/suprtype.cpp` | small | `SW.PostDependent` |
| `PreDependent` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Ares hardcodes this value and ignores whatever the rules file sets; SW.PostDependent replaces it. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/hardcoded.html) | `code/suprtype.cpp` | small | `SW.PostDependent` |
| `Light.Enabled` | rules: SuperWeaponType | If yes, the super weapon applies its own temporary lighting; only the three primary super weapons (Lightning Storm, Nuclear Missile, Psychic Dominator) support it. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/lighting.html) | `code/suprtype.cpp`, `code/lstorm.cpp`, `code/psydom.cpp` | medium | - |
| `Light.Ambient` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Ambient brightness during the effect, -1 for the scenario value; too high a value slows the game (MultiMissile defaults to 200). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/lighting.html) | `code/suprtype.cpp`, `code/lstorm.cpp`, `code/psydom.cpp` | small | `Light.Enabled` |
| `Light.Red` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Red lighting component during the effect, -1 for the scenario value (MultiMissile defaults to 175). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/lighting.html) | `code/suprtype.cpp`, `code/lstorm.cpp`, `code/psydom.cpp` | small | `Light.Enabled` |
| `Light.Green` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Green lighting component during the effect, -1 for the scenario value (MultiMissile defaults to 150). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/lighting.html) | `code/suprtype.cpp`, `code/lstorm.cpp`, `code/psydom.cpp` | small | `Light.Enabled` |
| `Light.Blue` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Blue lighting component during the effect, -1 for the scenario value (MultiMissile defaults to 125). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/lighting.html) | `code/suprtype.cpp`, `code/lstorm.cpp`, `code/psydom.cpp` | small | `Light.Enabled` |
| `Message.Detected` | rules: SuperWeaponType | CSF label shown to every player when the super weapon building is detected. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/messages.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/msglist.cpp` | small | - |
| `Message.Ready` | rules: SuperWeaponType | CSF label shown to the owner when the super weapon is ready. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/messages.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/msglist.cpp` | small | - |
| `Message.Launch` | rules: SuperWeaponType | CSF label shown to every player when the super weapon is launched. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/messages.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/msglist.cpp` | small | - |
| `Message.Activate` | rules: SuperWeaponType | CSF label shown to every player when a deferred super weapon takes effect. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/messages.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/msglist.cpp` | small | `SW.Deferment` |
| `Message.Abort` | rules: SuperWeaponType | CSF label shown to the firing player when the launch is refused because another super weapon is active. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/messages.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/msglist.cpp` | small | - |
| `Message.InsufficientFunds` | rules: SuperWeaponType | CSF label shown when the firing player lacks the money to launch. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/messages.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/msglist.cpp` | small | `Money.Amount` |
| `Message.CannotFire` | rules: SuperWeaponType | CSF label shown to the owner when firing with AI targeting finds no valid target; defaults to MSG:CannotFire. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/messages.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/msglist.cpp` | small | `SW.UseAITargeting` |
| `Message.FirerColor` | rules: SuperWeaponType | If yes, the Message.* texts use the firing house's colour scheme (default no). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/messages.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/msglist.cpp` | small | - |
| `Message.Color` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Colour scheme used for all Message.* texts instead of the player's; ignored when Message.FirerColor=yes. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/messages.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/msglist.cpp` | small | - |
| `SW.RangeMaximum` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Maximum distance in cells from a building providing the super weapon to the target; a negative value (default -1.0) uses the Type default, which usually disables the check, and at least one providing building must satisfy both range limits. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/range.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/house.cpp` | medium | - |
| `SW.RangeMinimum` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Minimum distance in cells from a providing building to the target; a negative value (default -1.0) uses the Type default, which usually disables the check (EMPulse defaults to 0.0). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/range.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/house.cpp` | medium | - |
| `SW.Designators` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Lists the units and structures of which one must be near the target for the super weapon to fire there; an empty list (default) needs no designator. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/range.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/techtype.cpp` | medium | `DesignatorRange` |
| `SW.AnyDesignator` | rules: SuperWeaponType | If yes, any unit or structure counts as a designator and SW.Designators is overridden (default no). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/range.html) | `code/super.cpp` | small | `DesignatorRange` |
| `SW.Inhibitors` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Lists enemy units and structures that stop the super weapon from firing near them; buildings only inhibit while powered and staffed as required (default none). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/range.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/techtype.cpp` | medium | `InhibitorRange` |
| `SW.AnyInhibitor` | rules: SuperWeaponType | If yes, any enemy unit or structure counts as an inhibitor and SW.Inhibitors is overridden (default no). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/range.html) | `code/super.cpp` | small | `InhibitorRange` |
| `SW.UseAITargeting` | rules: SuperWeaponType | If yes, clicking the cameo fires at an automatically chosen location instead of allowing manual targeting, and the cameo is darkened while the AI constraints fail; default no. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/targeting.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/house.cpp`, `code/sidebar.cpp` | medium | `SW.AITargeting`, `Message.CannotFire` |
| `SW.AutoFire` | rules: SuperWeaponType | If yes, the super weapon fires itself as soon as it is ready, even for human players, at the target the AI targeting options pick (default no). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/targeting.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/house.cpp` | medium | `SW.AITargeting` |
| `SW.ManualFire` | rules: SuperWeaponType | If no, clicking the cameo gives no target cursor; default yes, and the page says it is ignored when SW.AutoFire=no. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/targeting.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/sidebar.cpp` | small | `SW.AutoFire` |
| `SW.FireIntoShroud` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Whether a human player may fire the super weapon into unexplored shroud (default yes). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/targeting.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/mouse.cpp` | small | - |
| `SW.RequiresTarget` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Limits what a human player can fire at (none, land, water, empty, infantry, units, buildings or a comma list), showing NoCursor elsewhere; mutually exclusive settings with SW.AffectsTarget can leave the super weapon unable to affect anything. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/targeting.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/mouse.cpp` | medium | `SW.AffectsTarget` |
| `SW.RequiresHouse` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Limits whose objects a human player can fire at (none, owner, allies, team, enemies, all); defaults to team for ForceShield and none for IronCurtain. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/targeting.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/mouse.cpp` | medium | - |
| `SW.AITargeting` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Picks the automatic target selection mode from a fixed list (None, Nuke, LightningStorm, PsychicDominator, GeneticMutator, IronCurtain, ForceShield, ParaDrop, DropPod, Offensive, MultiMissile, HunterSeeker, NoTarget, Stealth, Self, Base, EnemyBase, Attack, LowPower, LowPowerAttack, LightningRandom); the default depends on Type. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/targeting.html) | `code/house.cpp`, `code/super.cpp` | large | `SW.AIRequiresTarget`, `SW.AIRequiresHouse`, `SW.AITargeting.Constraints`, `SW.AITargeting.Preference` |
| `SW.AIRequiresTarget` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Lists the target kinds AI players and auto-fired super weapons consider (none, land, water, empty, infantry, units, buildings, all); the default comes from a per-mode table, and setting it on a mode marked do-not-use can stop the super weapon from firing. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/targeting.html) | `code/house.cpp`, `code/super.cpp` | medium | `SW.AITargeting` |
| `SW.AIRequiresHouse` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Lists the houses AI players and auto-fired super weapons consider (owner, allies, team, enemies, others, all); the default comes from a per-mode table. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/targeting.html) | `code/house.cpp`, `code/super.cpp` | medium | `SW.AITargeting` |
| `SW.AITargeting.Constraints` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Comma list of conditions that must all hold before AI fires (None, Enemy, Offensive_Cell_Set, Offensive_Cell_Clear, Defensive_Cell_Set, Defensive_Cell_Clear, LightningStorm_Inactive, Dominator_Inactive, Attacked, LowPower); the last four do not apply to humans firing manually, and the default depends on Type. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/targeting.html) | `code/house.cpp`, `code/super.cpp` | medium | `SW.AITargeting` |
| `SW.AITargeting.Preference` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Overrides the target with the offensive cell (set by map action 135) or the defensive cell (set by map action 140) while one is set (None, Offensive, Defensive); the default depends on Type. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/targeting.html) | `code/house.cpp`, `code/super.cpp` | medium | `SW.AITargeting` |
| `SW.Damage` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Damage the effect deals, with Type defaults (GenericWarhead has none listed, GeneticConverter 10000, LightningStorm [General]LightningDamage, PsychicDominator [General]DominatorDamage with none dealt at 0 or below, MultiMissile -1 for the payload weapon's damage). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/genericwarhead.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | small | `SW.Warhead` |
| `SW.Warhead` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Warhead detonated for the effect, with Type defaults (GeneticConverter [SpecialWeapons]MutateWarhead or MutateExplosionWarhead, LightningStorm [General]LightningWarhead, PsychicDominator [General]DominatorWarhead, MultiMissile empty to use the payload weapon's warhead); for GenericWarhead it detonates in a cell, so the warhead needs a CellSpread to hit infantry. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/genericwarhead.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/warhead.cpp` | small | `SW.Damage` |
| `SW.Animation` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Animation shown at the target cell, with Type defaults (ChronoSphere [General]ChronoPlacement, GeneticConverter [General]IonBlast, IronCurtain [General]IronCurtainInvokeAnim, ForceShield [General]ForceShieldInvokeAnim). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/animsound.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | small | - |
| `SW.AnimationHeight` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Height above the target cell at which SW.Animation is shown (5 for ChronoSphere, GeneticConverter, IronCurtain and ForceShield). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/animsound.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | small | `SW.Animation` |
| `SW.AnimationVisibility` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Chooses who sees SW.Animation (none, owner, allies, team, enemies, all). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/animsound.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | small | `SW.Animation` |
| `SW.Sound` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Sound played at the target cell, with Type defaults (GeneticConverter [AudioVisual]GeneticMutatorActivateSound, PsychicReveal [AudioVisual]PsychicRevealActivateSound). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/animsound.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/voc.cpp` | small | - |
| `SW.ActivationSound` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Sound played when a nuke fires or a deferrable super weapon activates, usually at the target (LightningStorm [AudioVisual]StormSound, PsychicDominator [AudioVisual]PsychicDominatorActivateSound, MultiMissile [AudioVisual]DigSound, UnitDelivery none). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/animsound.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/voc.cpp` | small | - |
| `SW.MaxCount` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Maximum number of buildings that act when the super weapon fires (EMPulse cannons default 1, HunterSeeker launchers default 1); negative means unlimited. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/hunterseeker.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | small | - |
| `Type=Battery` | rules: SuperWeaponType | New Ares super weapon type that supplies auxiliary power while active, using charge/drain logic; SW.AITargeting defaults to LowPower. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/battery.html) | `code/suprtype.cpp`, `code/super.cpp`, `code/power.cpp` | large | `SW.ChargeToDrainRatio` |
| `Battery.Power` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Auxiliary power given to the owner while the Battery super weapon is active, independent of base damage (default 0). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/battery.html) | `code/power.cpp`, `code/house.cpp` | medium | `Type=Battery` |
| `Battery.Overpower` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Lists building types that are overpowered while active so they use the secondary weapon, with or without Overpowerable=yes (default none). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/battery.html) | `code/building.cpp`, `code/power.cpp` | medium | `Type=Battery` |
| `Battery.KeepOnline` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Lists building types kept powered up while active regardless of power level; some building effects may still fail at low power (default none). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/battery.html) | `code/building.cpp`, `code/power.cpp` | medium | `Type=Battery` |
| `Type=ChronoWarp` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Marks the destination of a chronoshift; only its targeting, cursor and Range-indicator properties are used and every ChronoSphere needs one. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/chronosphere.html) | `code/suprtype.cpp`, `code/super.cpp` | small | `SW.PostDependent` |
| `Chronosphere.BlastSrc` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Animation played over the source when the chronoshift starts; defaults to [General]ChronoBlast. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/chronosphere.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/anim.cpp` | small | - |
| `Chronosphere.BlastDest` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Animation played over the destination when the chronoshift starts; defaults to [General]ChronoBlastDest. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/chronosphere.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/anim.cpp` | small | - |
| `Chronosphere.ReconsiderBuildings` | rules: SuperWeaponType | If yes (default), buildings with Chronoshift.IsVehicle=yes are treated as vehicles; if no they always count as buildings like the original. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/chronosphere.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/building.cpp` | medium | `Chronoshift.IsVehicle` |
| `Chronosphere.KillOrganic` | rules: SuperWeaponType | If yes (default), the chronoshift kills organic units; if no they teleport. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/chronosphere.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/infantry.cpp` | small | - |
| `Chronosphere.KillTeleporters` | rules: SuperWeaponType | If yes, units with Teleporter=yes are killed by the chronoshift; default no, so they are shifted. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/chronosphere.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/techtype.cpp` | small | - |
| `Chronosphere.AffectsIronCurtain` | rules: SuperWeaponType | If yes, iron-curtained units are chronoshifted; default no, so they are ignored. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/chronosphere.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | - |
| `Chronosphere.AffectsUnwarpable` | rules: SuperWeaponType | If no, units with Warpable=no are ignored; default yes. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/chronosphere.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/techtype.cpp` | small | - |
| `Chronosphere.AffectsUndeployable` | rules: SuperWeaponType | If yes, a building with UndeploysInto= is chronoshifted with vehicle placement rules regardless of SW.AffectsTarget and Chronoshift.IsVehicle (default no). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/chronosphere.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/building.cpp` | medium | `Chronoshift.IsVehicle`, `SW.AffectsTarget` |
| `Chronosphere.BlowUnplaceable` | rules: SuperWeaponType | If yes (default), a building that does not fit at the destination blows up at the source, including units deployed into buildings; if no it stays at the source. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/chronosphere.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/building.cpp` | medium | - |
| `Chronosphere.KillCargo` | rules: SuperWeaponType | If yes, passengers and occupants die when their carrier is chronoshifted; default no. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/chronosphere.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/cargo.cpp` | small | - |
| `Type=DropPod` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Ares re-implementation of the Firestorm Drop Pod super weapon; it has no default unit types, so DropPod.Types or [General]DropPodTypes must be set. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/droppod.html) | `code/suprtype.cpp`, `code/super.cpp`, `code/droppod.cpp` | large | `DropPod.Types`, `DropPodTypes` |
| `DropPod.Types` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Infantry types picked at random, each equally likely and repeats allowed, for each pod; defaults to [General]DropPodTypes. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/droppod.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/droppod.cpp` | medium | `DropPodTypes` |

| `DropPod.Maximum` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Maximum number of drop pods created; defaults to [General]DropPodMaximum, and placement gives up after 3 times the unit count in retries. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/droppod.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/droppod.cpp` | small | `DropPodMaximum` |
| `Type=EMPulse` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Recreates the Tiberian Sun EMPulse: chosen cannon buildings fire at a target or at themselves together; the AI cannot use ranges for it yet. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/empulse.html) | `code/suprtype.cpp`, `code/super.cpp`, `code/empulse.cpp` | large | `EMPulse.Cannons` |
| `EMPulse.Cannons` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Building types that are range-checked and fire; an empty list (default) uses all buildings with EMPulseCannon=yes, and all of them need a primary weapon. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/empulse.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/building.cpp` | medium | `EMPulseCannon` |
| `EMPulse.TargetSelf` | rules: SuperWeaponType | If yes, each firing cannon detonates its primary weapon on itself once, like a death weapon, and only damages itself with DamageSelf=yes (default no). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/empulse.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/combat.cpp` | medium | `DamageSelf` |
| `EMPulse.Linked` | rules: SuperWeaponType | If yes, only one cannon needs the target in range and all other cannons fire regardless of range; only useful with SW.MaxCount other than 1 (default no). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/empulse.html) | `code/super.cpp` | small | `SW.MaxCount` |

| `EMPulse.PulseDelay` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Frames between the cannon facing the target (pulse ball starts) and firing; default 32. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/empulse.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/building.cpp` | small | `EMPulse.PulseBall` |
| `Type=Firestorm` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Recreates the Tiberian Sun Firestorm Defense System with charge/drain logic that Ares forces on regardless of UseChargeDrain, and it ignores the assigned Action so one cameo click activates it; the AI never uses it. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/firestorm.html) | `code/suprtype.cpp`, `code/super.cpp` | large | `SW.ChargeToDrainRatio` |
| `Type=GenericWarhead` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Detonates SW.Warhead with SW.Damage at the target cell (SW.AITargeting defaults to Offensive); the firing building needs DamageSelf=yes to hurt itself. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/genericwarhead.html) | `code/suprtype.cpp`, `code/super.cpp` | medium | `SW.Warhead`, `SW.Damage`, `DamageSelf` |
| `Mutate.Explosion` | rules: SuperWeaponType | If yes, the Genetic Mutator makes a plain warhead explosion with SW.Warhead and SW.Damage and ignores the other Mutate.* tags; if no it damages all infantry in range with verses and immunities applied; defaults to [General]MutateExplosion. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/geneticconverter.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/infantry.cpp` | medium | `SW.Warhead`, `SW.Damage` |
| `Mutate.IgnoreCyborg` | rules: SuperWeaponType | If yes, infantry with Cyborg=yes are unaffected; ignored with Mutate.Explosion=yes, default no. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/geneticconverter.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/infatype.cpp` | small | `Mutate.Explosion` |

| `Type=HunterSeeker` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Restored Tiberian Sun super weapon that launches up to SW.MaxCount Hunter Seeker vehicles, one per launch building, which steer into enemy objects and avoid Iron Curtained or warped ones; a seeker with Harvester=yes harvests instead. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/hunterseeker.html) | `code/suprtype.cpp`, `code/super.cpp`, `code/unit.cpp` | large | `HunterSeeker.Buildings`, `SW.MaxCount` |
| `HunterSeeker.Buildings` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Building types that can launch the Hunter Seeker, not necessarily those providing the super weapon; with none owned the super weapon discharges without a launch; defaults to [SpecialWeapons]HSBuilding. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/hunterseeker.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/rules.cpp` | medium | - |
| `HunterSeeker.Type` | rules: SuperWeaponType | VehicleType to spawn instead of the side's default Hunter Seeker. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/hunterseeker.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/side.cpp` | small | - |
| `HunterSeeker.RandomOnly` | rules: SuperWeaponType | If yes, every enemy object has an equal chance of being targeted; if no (default), human players in multiplayer prefer non-civilian targets and fall back to random. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/hunterseeker.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/unit.cpp` | small | - |

| `Lightning.RadarOutage` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Frames radars are jammed for houses selected by SW.AffectsHouse; defaults to [General]LightningStormDuration. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/lightningstorm.html) | `code/lstorm.cpp`, `code/radar.cpp` | small | `SW.AffectsHouse` |
| `Lightning.RadarOutageAffects` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Houses affected by the radar outage; default enemies. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/lightningstorm.html) | `code/lstorm.cpp`, `code/radar.cpp` | small | `Lightning.RadarOutage` |
| `Lightning.HitDelay` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Frames between clouds created directly over the target cell, 0 or below disabling direct hits; these clouds ignore separation rules; defaults to [General]LightningHitDelay. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/lightningstorm.html) | `code/lstorm.cpp` | small | - |
| `Lightning.ScatterDelay` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Frames between batches of clouds over random cells in range, 0 or below disabling random hits; only these clouds follow separation rules; defaults to [General]LightningScatterDelay. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/lightningstorm.html) | `code/lstorm.cpp` | small | `Lightning.ScatterCount` |
| `Lightning.ScatterCount` | rules: SuperWeaponType | New random clouds per scatter delay, 0 or below disabling random hits; default 1. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/lightningstorm.html) | `code/lstorm.cpp` | small | `Lightning.ScatterDelay` |
| `Lightning.Separation` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Least sum of x and y cell differences between two random clouds, 0 or below disabling the rule; defaults to [General]LightningSeparation. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/lightningstorm.html) | `code/lstorm.cpp` | small | - |
| `Lightning.PrintText` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Enables the warning text shortly before the storm strikes; defaults to [General]LightningPrintText. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/types/lightningstorm.html) | `code/lstorm.cpp`, `code/msglist.cpp` | small | - |

| `Berserk.ROFMultiplier` | rules: TechnoType | Multiplies the reload time of this unit while berserk (default `[CombatDamage]BerserkROFMultiplier`); smaller fires faster. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/berserk.html) | `code/techtype.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | `BerserkROFMultiplier` |
| `ImmuneToBerserk` | rules: TechnoType | Makes the object immune to Chaos Gas and `Psychedelic=yes` warheads, overriding `ImmuneToPsionics` and the PSIONICSIMMUNE ability either way; if unset, follows those two. Cannot be unset once set. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/berserk.html) | `code/techtype.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | `ImmuneToPsionics` |
| `TiberiumProof` | rules: TechnoType | Makes the unit immune to tiberium damage (default no for infantry, yes for everything else). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/tiberium/damage.html) | `code/techtype.cpp`, `code/foot.cpp`, `code/infantry.cpp` | small | `TiberiumDamageEnabled` |
| `TiberiumTransmogrify` | rules: TechnoType | Sets the percent chance this unit becomes a `[General]SmallVisceroid` when killed by tiberium damage (default `[General]TiberiumTransmogrify`). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/tiberium/damage.html) | `code/techtype.cpp`, `code/foot.cpp`, `code/infantry.cpp` | small | `TiberiumDamageEnabled`, `TiberiumDeathToVisceroid` |
| `SmallVisceroid` | rules: TechnoType | Lets two units with this flag that meet merge into a unit of type `[General]LargeVisceroid`; visceroids do not yet retreat to tiberium to regenerate. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/tiberium/damage.html) | `code/unit.cpp`, `code/unittype.cpp` | medium | `LargeVisceroid` |
| ✔ `TiberiumHeal` | rules: TechnoType | Makes the unit regain hitpoints while on a tiberium cell (default no); if yes, it also leaves tiberium when destroyed unless `TiberiumRemains` says otherwise. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/tiberium/heal.html) | `code/techtype.cpp`, `code/foot.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | medium | `TiberiumHealEnabled` |
| `TiberiumRemains` | rules: TechnoType | Makes the unit leave up to five tiberium bails in adjacent cells when destroyed (default `TiberiumHeal` if `[General]TiberiumHealEnabled=yes`, else no). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/tiberium/heal.html) | `code/techtype.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp`, `code/cell.cpp` | medium | `TiberiumHeal` |
| `TiberiumSpill` | rules: TechnoType | Makes a techno with positive `Storage` spill stored tiberium when destroyed (default no): buildings drop as many bails as stored, units up to 9 bails of the first Tiberiums type and no more than their Storage. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/tiberium/spill.html) | `code/techtype.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp`, `code/cell.cpp` | medium | `Storage` |
| `ReloadRate` | rules: AircraftType | Minutes between restoring one round while docked at a `UnitReload=yes` building; defaults to `[General]ReloadRate`, and each aircraft now reloads on its own timer. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/aircraftreload.html) | `code/aircraft.cpp`, `code/airctype.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | medium | `[General]ReloadRate` |
| `Smoke.Anim` | rules: AircraftType | Animation used for the smoke trail of a heavily damaged aircraft; defaults to `SGRYSMK1`. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/aircraftsmoke.html) | `code/aircraft.cpp`, `code/airctype.cpp` | small | - |
| `Smoke.ChanceRed` | rules: AircraftType | Per-frame percent chance that a heavily damaged aircraft spawns a smoke animation; defaults to 10. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/aircraftsmoke.html) | `code/aircraft.cpp`, `code/airctype.cpp` | small | - |
| `Smoke.ChanceDead` | rules: AircraftType | Per-frame percent chance that a crashing aircraft spawns a smoke animation; defaults to 80. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/aircraftsmoke.html) | `code/aircraft.cpp`, `code/airctype.cpp` | small | - |
| `AirRate` | art: TechnoType | Frames between animation steps while the unit is airborne (takeoff, landing and hovering included); a value above 0 overrides `WalkRate` and `IdleRate`, and 0 disables it (default). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/airrate.html) | `code/foot.cpp`, `code/techtype.cpp` | small | - |
| `AlternateTheaterArt` | art: TechnoType | Lets an SHP-based unit load an art section with a theater letter appended (for example `JUNKA` on arctic, `JUNKD` on desert) and fall back to the plain section if it is missing; defaults to no and does not work for voxels. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/alternatetheaterart.html) | `code/techtype.cpp`, `code/theater.cpp` | medium | - |
| ✔ `AttachEffect.Delay` | rules: TechnoType | Frames after the previous effect ends before the unit re-creates its own effect; a negative value never renews it, and 0 (default) renews at once. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/attacheffect.html) | `code/attacheffect.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | medium | `AttachEffect.Duration` |

| ✔ `Doggie` | rules: InfantryType | Boolean for Tiberian Fiend behaviour (sits on Tiberium in guard mode, flees to Tiberium when badly hurt, is easily aggravated); needs `Crawls=yes` to sit and `NotHuman=yes` to avoid human death animations, default no. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/doggie.html) | `code/infantry.cpp`, `code/infatype.cpp` | medium | `Crawls`, `NotHuman` |
| `Drain.Local` | rules: TechnoType | Boolean; if either the draining unit or the drained building sets it, only that building's local power is drained instead of the owner's whole grid, and the default is no. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/drain.html) | `code/building.cpp`, `code/house.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | medium | - |
| `Drain.Amount` | rules: TechnoType | Power drained from a victim power plant when `Drain.Local` applies; positive is a maximum, negative a minimum, applied first from the drainer and then the building, and the default is 0. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/drain.html) | `code/building.cpp`, `code/house.cpp` | medium | `Drain.Local` |
