| `FactoryPlant.Multiplier` | rules: TechnoType | Multiplier on this type's factory-plant cost bonus, from -1.0 (reverses it) to 1.0 (unchanged) and 0.0 (no bonus), with a bonus of 0.6 and 0.5 giving 0.8; defaults to 1.0 and values above 1.0 can drive the cost to zero. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/factoryplant.html) | `code/house.cpp`, `code/builtype.cpp`, `code/techtype.cpp` | small | `FactoryPlant` |

| `FallRate.Parachute` | rules: TechnoType | Per-frame downward acceleration of a parachuting unit; defaults to 1. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/fallrate.html) | `code/object.cpp`, `code/techtype.cpp` | small | - |
| `FallRate.NoParachute` | rules: TechnoType | Per-frame downward acceleration of a unit falling without a parachute; defaults to 1. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/fallrate.html) | `code/object.cpp`, `code/techtype.cpp` | small | - |
| `FallRate.ParachuteMax` | rules: TechnoType | Maximum fall speed of a parachuting unit (a negative number); defaults to `[General]ParachuteMaxFallRate`. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/fallrate.html) | `code/object.cpp`, `code/techtype.cpp` | small | `[General]ParachuteMaxFallRate` |

| `Gunner` | rules: VehicleType | Every `Gunner=yes` vehicle now reads the special turret and weapon flags such as `SniperTurretIndex`, so several IFV types can exist. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/gunner.html) | `code/unit.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp`, `code/unittype.cpp` | medium | - |
| `TurretCount` | rules: VehicleType | Number of turret and barrel voxels, now allowed above 18 up to 127, together with more than 18 weapons and `IFVMode` values. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/gunner.html) | `code/unit.cpp`, `code/voxel.cpp`, `code/techtype.cpp` | medium | - |
| `WeaponTurretIndexX` | rules: TechnoType | Turret index used when `WeaponX` and `EliteWeaponX` are active through the passenger's 0-based `IFVMode` (so `IFVMode=4` uses `WeaponTurretIndex5`); defaults to -1 and invalid indices are logged. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/gunner.html) | `code/unit.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | medium | `Gunner`, `IFVMode` |
| `WeaponUINameX` | rules: TechnoType | CSF label shown as the tooltip of a `Gunner=yes` unit while weapon X is active; unset keeps the original passenger-and-transport name logic. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/gunner.html) | `code/tooltip.cpp`, `code/unit.cpp` | small | `Gunner` |

| `IsCow` | rules: InfantryType | Boolean that gives the infantry the cow behaviour of playing its idle animation more often and wandering randomly; defaults to yes for `COW`, otherwise no. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/hardcodedunitproperties.html) | `code/infantry.cpp`, `code/infatype.cpp` | small | - |
| `IsDesolator` | rules: InfantryType | Boolean for the Desolator's deploy-weapon firing timing and the automatic ground fire when deployed with `ImmuneToRadiation=yes`; defaults to yes for `DESO`, otherwise no. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/hardcodedunitproperties.html) | `code/infantry.cpp`, `code/infatype.cpp` | small | `ImmuneToRadiation` |

| `Harvester.ShortScan` | rules: TechnoType | Distance in cells for short scans; defaults to `TiberiumShortScan` for harvesters, `SlaveMinerShortScan` for slave miners and `SlaveMinerSlaveScan` for slaves. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/harvesters.html) | `code/unit.cpp`, `code/slaveman.cpp`, `code/rules.cpp` | small | - |
| `Harvester.TooFarDistance` | rules: VehicleType | Distance in cells beyond which a refinery is too far to reserve, needing `Harvester=yes`; defaults to `ChronoHarvTooFarDistance` for `Teleporter=yes` units and `HarvesterTooFarDistance` otherwise. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/harvesters.html) | `code/unit.cpp`, `code/rules.cpp` | small | - |
| `Harvester.KickDelay` | rules: VehicleType | Frames between automatic wake-ups of a slave miner, -1 for none; defaults to `[General]SlaveMinerKickFrameDelay`. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/harvesters.html) | `code/slaveman.cpp`, `code/unit.cpp` | small | - |
| `VehicleThief.EnterSound` | rules: InfantryType | Sound played when a hijacker captures an enemy vehicle, also for `CanDrive=yes` infantry reclaiming neutralised units; defaults to none. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/hijackers.html) | `code/infantry.cpp`, `code/infatype.cpp`, `code/voc.cpp` | small | `VehicleThief` |

| `HunterSeeker.DetonateProximity` | rules: VehicleType | Per-unit detonation distance in leptons; defaults to `[General]HunterSeekerDetonateProximity`, and only the Fly locomotor uses it. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/hunterseeker.html) | `code/fly.cpp`, `code/techtype.cpp` | small | `HunterSeeker` |

| `InitialPayload.Types` | rules: TechnoType | List of TechnoTypes the object is created holding (buildings need `CanBeOccupied=yes` or `InfantryAbsorb=yes` and take infantry only; infantry, aircraft and types with a payload themselves are invalid); repeats are allowed and capacity and size limits are not checked. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/initialpayload.html) | `code/cargo.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp`, `code/techtype.cpp` | medium | `Passengers`, `MaxNumberOccupants` |

| `ProtectedDriver` | rules: TechnoType | Makes the vehicle's driver immune to KillDriver warheads; Organic and Natural units are always immune (default no). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/killingdrivers.html) | `code/techtype.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | KillDriver, ProtectedDriver.MinHealth |

| `CanBeDriven` | rules: Country | Whether units owned by this country can be taken over by CanDrive infantry (default: the value of MultiplayPassive for the country). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/killingdrivers.html) | `code/houstype.cpp`, `code/infantry.cpp` | small | CanDrive |

| `PSIONICWEAPONIMMUNE` | rules: VeteranAbilities / EliteAbilities | Ability making the unit immune to PsychicDamage weapons, like ImmuneToPsionicWeapons=yes. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/veteranabilities.html) | `code/veteran.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp`, `code/psydom.cpp` | small | - |

| `EVA.Lost` | rules: TechnoType | EVA message played when a unit or structure of this type is destroyed; none disables it (default none for buildings, EVA_UnitLost otherwise); not played for DontScore, Insignificant or Spawned objects. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/losteva.html) | `code/vox.cpp`, `code/techtype.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | - |
| `NoManualFire` | rules: TechnoType | Stops players from picking targets for this object by hand; auto-targeting and retaliation still work (default no). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/manual.html) | `code/techtype.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | - |
| `NoManualUnload` | rules: TechnoType | Stops players from ejecting the passengers of this vehicle or aircraft by hand and hides the deploy cursors (default no). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/manual.html) | `code/techtype.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | Survivor.RookiePassengerChance |
| `NoManualEnter` | rules: TechnoType | Hides the enter and no-enter cursors over this object so it looks like a non-transport; scripts and the AI can still load it (default no). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/manual.html) | `code/techtype.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | Passengers.Allowed |

| `PrismForwarding.Targets` | rules: BuildingType | Lists the BuildingTypes this tower may support (default only its own type, so list the tower itself when overriding). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/buildings/prismforwarding.html) | `code/building.cpp`, `code/builtype.cpp` | small | `PrismForwarding` |

| `Rubble.Destroyed.Strength` | rules: BuildingType | Sets the rubble health: positive values up to Strength are used directly, -1 to -99 are a percentage of full health, anything else is full health (default Strength). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/buildings/urbancombattrenches.html) | `code/building.cpp`, `code/builtype.cpp` | small | `Rubble.Destroyed` |
| `Rubble.Destroyed.Anim` | rules: BuildingType | Animation played when the building becomes rubble or is removed (default none). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/buildings/urbancombattrenches.html) | `code/building.cpp`, `code/builtype.cpp`, `code/anim.cpp` | small | `Rubble.Destroyed` |
| `Rubble.Intact` | rules: BuildingType | Names the BuildingType this rubble turns into when an Engineer repairs it, at 1% Strength unless set otherwise, without consuming the Engineer. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/buildings/urbancombattrenches.html) | `code/building.cpp`, `code/builtype.cpp`, `code/infantry.cpp` | large | `Rubble.Destroyed` |
| `Rubble.Intact.Remove` | rules: BuildingType | Removes the rubble instead of recovering a building when an Engineer enters (default no). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/buildings/urbancombattrenches.html) | `code/building.cpp`, `code/builtype.cpp` | small | `Rubble.Intact` |
| `Rubble.Intact.Owner` | rules: BuildingType | Sets the owner of the recovered building as default, civilian, special or neutral (default default). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/buildings/urbancombattrenches.html) | `code/building.cpp`, `code/builtype.cpp` | small | `Rubble.Intact` |
| `Rubble.Intact.Strength` | rules: BuildingType | Sets the recovered building health using the same positive, negative-percentage and full-health rules as the destroyed side (default -1, meaning 1%). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/buildings/urbancombattrenches.html) | `code/building.cpp`, `code/builtype.cpp` | small | `Rubble.Intact` |
| `Rubble.Intact.Anim` | rules: BuildingType | Animation played when the building is recovered or removed (default none). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/buildings/urbancombattrenches.html) | `code/building.cpp`, `code/builtype.cpp`, `code/anim.cpp` | small | `Rubble.Intact` |
| `IsTrench` | rules: BuildingType | Names the trench type so occupants can move between adjacent 1x1 buildings with the same name via an enter cursor; the docs say there is no special image joining yet. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/buildings/urbancombattrenches.html) | `code/building.cpp`, `code/builtype.cpp`, `code/infantry.cpp` | large | `CanBeOccupiedBy` |
| `CanBeOccupiedBy` | rules: BuildingType | Lists the InfantryTypes allowed to garrison the building, which also needs CanBeOccupied=yes and Occupier=yes on the infantry. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/buildings/urbancombattrenches.html) | `code/building.cpp`, `code/builtype.cpp`, `code/infantry.cpp` | small | - |
| `Tunnel` | rules: BuildingType | Makes the building an entrance to the named TunnelType (no default); entrances may not have weapons, turrets or capture and mind control, and show pips for the shared contents. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/buildings/tunnels.html) | `code/building.cpp`, `code/builtype.cpp`, `code/tunnel.cpp` | large | `Passengers`, `MaxSize` |
| ✔ `SensorArray` | rules: BuildingType | Detects stealth and subterranean units within SensorsSight (not the Cloak Generator range) with a matching radial indicator, and now stops when the array is destroyed and respects power, capture, mind control and temporal weapons (the docs only say these "should work now"). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/cloak/sensorarray.html) | `code/building.cpp`, `code/builtype.cpp`, `code/foot.cpp` | medium | `SensorsSight` |
| `Cloakable.Powered` | rules: BuildingType | When yes the building uncloaks when shut down or in low power; otherwise it may stay cloaked (default no), and Cloak Generator cloaking is unaffected. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/cloak/cloakstates.html) | `code/building.cpp`, `code/builtype.cpp` | small | - |
| `Refinery.UseStorage` | rules: BuildingType | Makes a refinery store dumped tiberium for silos instead of converting it to money at once (default no); ore purifier bonus money always goes to the bank, and undeployable refineries like the Slave Miner are unsupported. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/tiberium/storage.html) | `code/building.cpp`, `code/storage.cpp`, `code/house.cpp` | large | `Storage`, `EVA_SilosNeeded` |
| ✔ `PipScale` | rules: BuildingType | Accepts the value `Tiberium` again: pips show used storage out of total, with ore yellow and gems blue; on refineries and `ResourceDestination=yes` buildings it also needs `Refinery.UseStorage=yes`. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/tiberium/storage.html) | `code/building.cpp`, `code/builtype.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | medium | `Refinery.UseStorage` |
| `EnemyUIName` | rules: BuildingType | CSF label shown as the name to enemy players instead of `UIName`; observers, allies and players who infiltrated the building see the real name, and only buildings support it. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/enemyuiname.html) | `code/building.cpp`, `code/tooltip.cpp`, `code/csf.cpp` | small | `UIName` |
| `Harvester.ScanCorrection` | rules: BuildingType | Distance in cells considered better when a slave miner scoots forward, for slave miner buildings only; defaults to `[General]SlaveMinerScanCorrection`. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/harvesters.html) | `code/building.cpp`, `code/slaveman.cpp` | small | - |
| `FactoryOwners.Permanent` | rules: BuildingType | When yes, a player who captures this building keeps all plans of its initial owner permanently; works on upgrades too (default no). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/prerequisites.html) | `code/builtype.cpp`, `code/building.cpp`, `code/house.cpp` | medium | FactoryOwners |
| ✔ `ReverseEngineersVictims` | rules: BuildingType | Turns on reverse engineering of infantry or vehicles sent into this building, granting their build options and playing EVA_ReverseEngineeredInfantry or Vehicle and EVA_NewTechnologyAcquired the first time; currently needs Grinding=yes and is described as having minor bugs (default no). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/reverseengineerlogic.html) | `code/builtype.cpp`, `code/building.cpp`, `code/infantry.cpp` | large | Grinding, CanBeReversed, ReversedAs |
| `ImmuneToSaboteurs` | rules: BuildingType | Whether Saboteurs cannot enter this building (default yes for CanC4=no, and for unbuildable TechLevel=-1 CanBeOccupied=yes buildings; no otherwise). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/saboteur.html) | `code/builtype.cpp`, `code/building.cpp` | small | Saboteur |
| `UnitSell` | rules: BuildingType | Whether docked units can be sold at this building by the Sell button (cursor named SellUnit) (default: the value of UnitRepair). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/sellunit.html) | `code/builtype.cpp`, `code/building.cpp` | small | UnitRepair |
| `Crew.EngineerChance` | rules: BuildingType | Percent chance (0-100) that the side's Engineer is spawned instead of ordinary crew; never for captured buildings (default 25 for Factory=BuildingType, else 0). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/survivors.html) | `code/building.cpp`, `code/builtype.cpp` | small | Engineer, SurvivorDivisor |
| ✔ `GapRadiusInCells` | rules: BuildingType | Gap generator radius in cells; values above 127 are now accepted (the page warns that large values lag). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type2/gapradius.html) | `code/building.cpp`, `code/techtype.cpp` | small | - |
| `SuperGapRadiusInCells` | rules: BuildingType | Radius in cells for the super gap generator; values above 127 are now accepted. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type2/gapradius.html) | `code/building.cpp`, `code/techtype.cpp` | small | - |

## Animations and particles

| Tag | Section or type | Effect | Source | Files in `code/` | Size | Depends on |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `Weapon` | art: AnimType | Weapon whose projectile is created and detonated at once to deliver the animation's damage, with the damage amount still taken from the animation; the projectile has no owner, so scoring is not credited, and the weapon should be in `[WeaponTypes]`. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/animations.html) | `code/anim.cpp`, `code/animtype.cpp`, `code/bullet.cpp` | medium | `Damage` |
| `Damage.Delay` | art: AnimType | Animation frames between two applications of the animation's damage; 0 means no delay (default), and it needs `Damage` of at least 1.0. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/animations.html) | `code/anim.cpp`, `code/animtype.cpp` | small | `Damage` |
| ✔ `CustomPalette` | art: AnimType | Palette file for drawing this animation or projectile, with a three-character theater marker in the name replaced by the theater extension; a projectile using it must also be listed in `[Animations]`, and the page gives no default. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/customanimationandprojectilepalettes.html) | `code/anim.cpp`, `code/animtype.cpp`, `code/bullettype.cpp` | medium | - |
| `DamageSparks` | rules: TechnoType | Boolean that lets an object spawn spark particle systems from `DamageParticleSystems` while in yellow or red health; defaults to the value of `Cyborg` for infantry and no otherwise. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/damageparticlesystems.html) | `code/particle.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp`, `code/infatype.cpp` | medium | `DamageParticleSystems` |
| `DamageSmokeParticleSystems` | rules: TechnoType | List of particle systems randomly spawned when the object is damaged into yellow or red health; defaults to all `DamageParticleSystems` with `BehavesLike=Smoke`, and only Smoke and Spark behaviours are fully supported. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/damageparticlesystems.html) | `code/particle.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | medium | `DamageParticleSystems` |
| `DamageSparksParticleSystems` | rules: TechnoType | List of particle systems randomly spawned while the object is in yellow or red health; defaults to all `DamageParticleSystems` with `BehavesLike=Spark`, and only Smoke and Spark behaviours are fully supported. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/damageparticlesystems.html) | `code/particle.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | medium | `DamageParticleSystems` |
| `MakeInfantryOwner` | art: AnimType | Chooses which house owns the infantry created by MakeInfantry after the animation: invoker, killer, victim, civilian, special, neutral or random (default invoker); on chained animations (Next=) it goes on the first animation. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/makeinfantryowner.html) | `code/anim.cpp`, `code/animtype.cpp`, `code/infantry.cpp` | medium | MakeInfantry, Next |
| `DamageRange` | rules: ParticleType | Radius in cells in which a Gas particle damages objects, each at most once; 0.0 or less affects only the particle's own cell (default 0.0). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/particles.html) | `code/ptype.cpp`, `code/particle.cpp`, `code/combat.cpp` | medium | BehavesLike |
| `Palette` | rules: ParticleType | Palette file (.pal) used to draw shape particles of types with BehavesLike Gas, Smoke or Fire (default ANIM.PAL). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/particles.html) | `code/ptype.cpp`, `code/particle.cpp`, `code/palette.cpp` | medium | BehavesLike |
| ✔ `Scorch` | art: AnimType | When yes, a SmallFire animation is attached to the same object when this animation ends, except on water, beach, ice or rock; do not set it on SmallFire itself (default no). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/scorchflamer.html) | `code/anim.cpp`, `code/animtype.cpp` | medium | SmallFire |
| ✔ `Flamer` | art: AnimType | When yes, SmallFire and LargeFire animations are created at random near this animation's location when it ends (default no). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/scorchflamer.html) | `code/anim.cpp`, `code/animtype.cpp` | medium | SmallFire, LargeFire |
| `SpawnsParticle.RangeMinimum` | rules: AnimType | Minimum distance in cells from the animation's centre at which spawned particles appear; a negative value can give negative random distances, which use the absolute value (default 0.0). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/spawnsparticle.html) | `code/animtype.cpp`, `code/anim.cpp` | small | SpawnsParticle |
| `SpawnsParticle.RangeMaximum` | rules: AnimType | Maximum distance in cells from the centre for spawned particles; must be at least the minimum (default 0.0). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/spawnsparticle.html) | `code/animtype.cpp`, `code/anim.cpp` | small | SpawnsParticle.RangeMinimum |
| `Warhead` | rules: AnimationType | The animation's warhead is now honoured; if unset the animation uses `[CombatDamage]FlameDamage2`, or `[CombatDamage]C4Warhead` for the animation `INVISO`, as the original hardcoded. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type2/animationdamagewarheads.html) | `code/anim.cpp`, `code/animtype.cpp` | small | `[CombatDamage]FlameDamage2`, `[CombatDamage]C4Warhead` |
| `AffectsAllies` | rules: AnimationType | An animation with an owner now also respects this flag; before only `AffectsEnemies` was supported (Ares 0.3 and below migration). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/migration.html) | `code/anim.cpp`, `code/animtype.cpp` | small | `AffectsEnemies` |

| `TiberiumChainReaction (Tiberium Explosion)` | rules: AnimType | Restores the Tiberian Sun effect where an animation with this flag hitting a tiberium cell may spawn debris and deal explosion damage. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/tiberium/chainreactions.html) | `code/anim.cpp`, `code/tiberium.cpp` | medium | `ExplosionWarhead`, `ExplosionDamage`, `Debris.Chance` |
| `ChainReaction (overlay)` | rules: OverlayType | Restores Tiberian Sun behaviour where damaged overlay spawns an animation on neighbouring cells that propagates the effect. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/tiberium/chainreactions.html) | `code/overlay.cpp`, `code/overtype.cpp`, `code/anim.cpp` | medium | `ExplosionWarhead` |
| `Cross-category heal and repair` | rules: WarheadType (negative Damage) | Lets infantry heal and vehicles repair any TechnoType according to Verses, enabling mechanics and ambulances; aircraft on a helipad or airport cannot be repaired by vehicles, though infantry can. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/mechanical.html) | `code/techno.cpp`, `code/infantry.cpp`, `code/unit.cpp` | medium | - |
| `Guard Area / Force Move heal override` | rules: mission and input | Guard Area makes units follow and protect a unit, and holding the Force Move keys overrides healing so units can enter damaged transporters; the page names no tag for either. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/warheads/mechanical.html) | `code/techno.cpp`, `code/mission.cpp` | medium | - |
| `FAData.ini` | FinalAlert2 | Gets the Ares trigger action and event definitions merged in from `FADataCode.txt` (by Starkku), and any custom ParamTypes already added may need updating. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/editorsupport.html) | `code/techno.cpp` | small | - |
| `FA2Ext.dll` | FinalAlert2 | Third-party extension, not bundled with Ares, needed to reach the new team scripts from the editor interface. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/editorsupport.html) | `code/techno.cpp` | small | - |
| `[EVATypes]` | evamd.ini | List of new EVA announcer names (key text ignored) added to the built-in `Allied`, `Russian` and `Yuri` voices. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/evatypes.html) | `code/vox.cpp`, `code/rules.cpp` | medium | - |
| `<EVAType>=<filename>` | evamd.ini: EVA sound sections | Per announcer entry in an EVA sound section, the sound file (up to eight characters) it plays; with no entry nothing plays. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/evatypes.html) | `code/vox.cpp` | medium | `[EVATypes]` |
| `[Movies]` | moviemd.ini | Ordered list of movie file names (without .BIK) shown in the in-game movie menu; if empty the game keeps the hardcoded lists and the two campaign progress trackers. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/movies.html) | `code/movies.cpp`, `code/campmovies.cpp` | large | Description, Unlockable |
| `[UnlockedMovies]` | ra2md.ini | New section that stores which movies are unlocked, replacing the two Allied and Soviet progress trackers. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/movies.html) | `code/movies.cpp`, `code/campmovies.cpp`, `code/ccini.cpp` | medium | [Movies] |
| `Description` | moviemd.ini: movie section | CSF label shown for the movie in the list; required for each movie. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/movies.html) | `code/movies.cpp`, `code/campmovies.cpp` | small | [Movies] |
| `Unlockable` | moviemd.ini: movie section | Whether the movie must be unlocked before it appears in the list; no means it is always listed (default yes). | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/movies.html) | `code/movies.cpp`, `code/campmovies.cpp` | small | [Movies] |
| `Particle optimization (no tag)` | rules: ParticleSystemType / ParticleType | Spark, Railgun and Smoke particles use a faster path automatically when the system's BehavesLike and its HoldsWhat particle have the same value and the particle uses no alpha images or line trailers; mixing Spark and Railgun disables it. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/particles.html) | `code/partsys.cpp`, `code/psystype.cpp`, `code/particle.cpp`, `code/ptype.cpp` | large | BehavesLike, HoldsWhat |
| `[GenericPrerequisites]` | rules: new section | New section for custom prerequisite groups; a group named POWER, FACTORY, BARRACKS, RADAR, TECH or PROC replaces the matching [General] Prerequisite list, except PROC does not replace PrerequisiteProcAlternate. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/prerequisites.html) | `code/rules.cpp`, `code/house.cpp`, `code/techtype.cpp` | medium | Prerequisites |
| `GROUPNAME` | rules: [GenericPrerequisites] | Defines a prerequisite group as a list of buildings, usable by name in a type's Prerequisites=. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/prerequisites.html) | `code/rules.cpp`, `code/house.cpp`, `code/techtype.cpp` | medium | [GenericPrerequisites] |
| `Sight above 10 (no tag)` | rules: TechnoType Sight | Sight values above 10 are accepted and give an octagonal view area; the page warns that large values cost performance and the method may change. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/sight.html) | `code/techtype.cpp`, `code/map.cpp`, `code/object.cpp` | medium | Sight |
| `Spawner cloak and BalloonHover support (no tag)` | rules: spawner units | Cloaking spawners no longer destroy their spawns, and spawns launch from and return to flying (BalloonHover) carriers; the page lists known shortcomings such as move sounds. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/spawners.html) | `code/spawnman.cpp`, `code/unit.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | large | BalloonHover, FlightLevel |
| `Type conversion rules (no tag)` | rules: TechnoType | Conversion works only unit to unit, infantry to infantry and aircraft to aircraft; health stays, ammo is cut to the new maximum, and prerequisites are rechecked; converted units stop counting as originals for BuildLimit. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/typeconversion.html) | `code/unit.cpp`, `code/infantry.cpp`, `code/aircraft.cpp`, `code/house.cpp` | large | - |
| `[Sides] and [Countries] lists (limits)` | rules.ini | Allows up to 16 fully working sides and countries, up to 32 total countries with taunts working for 16; playable countries should be listed first, Multiplay=yes countries show in the selection list. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/sidescountries/index.html) | `code/side.cpp`, `code/houstype.cpp`, `code/house.cpp` | large | Multiplay |

| `-NOLOGO` | command line | Skips the EA logo video before loading. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/ui-features/commandlinearguments.html) | `code/startup.cpp`, `code/init.cpp` | small | - |
| `-LOG` | command line | Starts with debug.log writing on. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/ui-features/commandlinearguments.html) | `code/dbgprint.cpp`, `code/startup.cpp` | small | - |
| `-LOG-CSF` | command line | Writes each CSF label not found in the language files once to debug.log, prefixed `[CSFLoader]`. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/ui-features/commandlinearguments.html) | `code/csf.cpp`, `code/dbgprint.cpp` | small | `-LOG` |
| `-STRICT` | command line | Raises an Internal Error at load for non-critical errors such as an unparseable non-empty value; the page warns stock rules trip it. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/ui-features/commandlinearguments.html) | `code/ccini.cpp`, `code/except.cpp` | medium | - |
| `-AI-CONTROL` | command line | Lets the player bind a hotkey that hands control to the AI; unsupported according to the page. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/ui-features/commandlinearguments.html) | `code/init.cpp`, `code/house.cpp` | medium | - |
| `-AFFINITY:N` | command line | Bit mask of processors the game may run on; default 1 (first processor only), 0 turns the feature off. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/ui-features/commandlinearguments.html) | `code/startup.cpp` | small | - |
